{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":[{"text":"Done. All requirements verified.\n\nThe migration was two reverted regressions plus one structural fix. The working tree had LTO enabled for *any* optimization except `-O0`, and it had `BUILD_TLS=yes` wrapped in an `ifeq ($(OPTIMIZATION),-O3)` guard — so TLS silently compiled with no `USE_OPENSSL` and no `libhiredis_ssl` at any other optimization level. I restored the LTO condition to fire exactly on `-O3`, removed the TLS guard, and set `OPTIMIZATION?=-O2`.\n\nThe structural part was necessary, not cosmetic. `.make-settings` is included *after* the original LTO block and re-assigns `OPT` and `REDIS_CFLAGS`, and those persisted values beat the command line. Simply flipping the default to `-O2` would have left `make OPTIMIZATION=-O3` building at `-O2` with no LTO after any prior build, and a persisted empty `REDIS_CFLAGS` would have stripped `-flto` from later `-O3` builds. So I moved the LTO flags into separate `LTO_CFLAGS`/`LTO_LDFLAGS` derived from the effective `OPT` after the include, and made a command-line `OPTIMIZATION` override a persisted `OPT`. I also added `export BUILD_TLS`: a `BUILD_TLS` restored from `.make-settings` was not reaching the deps sub-make that builds `libhiredis_ssl.a`, which is exactly the \"later make silently drops TLS\" failure the spec warns about.\n\nWhat I confirmed by building rather than by reading flags:\n\n- Default objects report `-O2` in their DWARF producer string, and 0 of 98 carry `.gnu.lto` sections; `-O3` objects carry them in all 98.\n- `/results/redis-server-tls --version` runs and links `libssl.so.3`/`libcrypto.so.3`, with 51 undefined `SSL_`/`OPENSSL_` symbols — TLS is genuinely compiled in, not just flagged.\n- `/results/redis-tls.so` is a 226112-byte ELF `DYN`.\n- After `make BUILD_TLS=yes`, a plain `make` was a no-op and the server kept its OpenSSL linkage.\n- jemalloc remains the Linux default; `noopt`/`valgrind`-style invocations still resolve correctly.\n\n`/results/build_flags.env` has the six keys in the required order, ASCII, trailing newline. One note on a deliberate choice: I left upstream's sticky-settings semantics intact, so a plain `make` after `make OPTIMIZATION=-O3` still builds at `-O3`. That's existing Redis behavior and the same mechanism the spec asks me to preserve for `BUILD_TLS`, so I ran the default build last from a `make distclean` tree — `/app/src` is now in the default `-O2`, no-LTO state.","type":"text"}],"id":"msg_de9ff52d4eda46c3a2b1bca03c4e32f6","model":"claude-opus-5","role":"assistant","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"type":"message","usage":{"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"input_tokens":20013,"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"49e738ee-01cf-4500-a7ef-b369c2a50f47","uuid":"d17235a1-af43-4156-b700-dd0465eb3d7a","timestamp":"2026-09-19T08:25:57.235Z"}
